It was more like I felt really energized, but not like I was hallucinating.\n\nDoctor: Have you heard voices or seen things that other people couldn't since you started feeling this heavy, hollow feeling?\n\nPatient: No, I haven’t noticed anything like that. I haven’t been hearing voices or seeing things other people couldn’t. It’s more just been this heavy feeling.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced any strange or unusual thoughts since you started feeling this heavy, hollow feeling?\n\nPatient: I’m not really sure what would count as strange or unusual. I haven’t noticed anything like, clearly bizarre thoughts. It’s mostly just felt empty and heavy, if that makes sense.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever heard voices or seen things that other people couldn't when you were feeling this heavy, hollow feeling?\n\nPatient: No, not that I can remember. When I’ve felt that hollow, heavy feeling, I haven’t been hearing voices or seeing things others couldn’t.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had thoughts that you think aren't your own when you're feeling this heavy, hollow feeling?\n\nPatient: No, I don’t think so. I haven’t had the sense that thoughts are being put in my head or that they’re not mine. It’s more like my own thoughts just feel heavy or hard to sit with.\n\nDoctor: Did you engage in any risky or impulsive behaviors during that three-week period when you felt extremely happy and energetic?\n\nPatient: I’m not really sure. I don’t remember doing anything clearly risky or impulsive during that time. I was just feeling really up and energized, but I can’t think of a specific behavior like that.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced any hallucinations since you started feeling this heavy, hollow feeling?\n\nPatient: No, I haven’t noticed anything like that since this started. I don’t think I’ve been seeing or hearing things that aren’t there.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced any hallucinations or delusions since you started feeling this heavy, hollow feeling?\n\nPatient: No, I don’t think so. I haven’t had hallucinations, and I don’t think I’ve believed things that weren’t real. It’s just been that heavy, empty feeling.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing.
